  17: import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
  18: import org.springframework.transaction.PlatformTransactionManager;
  19: import org.springframework.transaction.support.TransactionTemplate;
  20: 
  21: /**
  22:  * Automatic, token-free repair of authoritative local candle evidence.  It is
  23:  * deliberately not an analysis or execution service: recovered candles obtain
  24:  * SKIPPED_NOT_LIVE outcome evidence and wait for a subsequent genuine live
  25:  * close to resume ordinary analysis.
  26:  */
  27: @Service
  28: public class LocalCandleReconciliationService {
  29:     private static final org.slf4j.Logger log=org.slf4j.LoggerFactory.getLogger(LocalCandleReconciliationService.class);
  30:     private final JdbcTemplate jdbc;
  31:     private final CoinConfigurationService coins;
  32:     private final BinanceMarketDataProperties properties;
  33:     private final BinanceMarketDataClient binance;
  34:     private final CandleRepository candles;
  35:     private final LocalReconciliationAuditRepository audit;
  36:     private final CandleAnalysisOutcomeStore outcomes;
  37:     private final Executor executor;
  38:     private final TransactionTemplate tx;
  39:     private final AtomicBoolean running=new AtomicBoolean();
  40:     private volatile Instant lastReconnectAdmission;
  41:     @org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired(required=false) private LocalDrainService drain;
  42:     @org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired(required=false) private ExchangeClockSynchronizer exchangeClock;
  43:     @Value("${shared-market.mode:OFF}") private String mode;
  44:     @Value("${local-candle.reconciliation.enabled:false}") private boolean enabled;
  45:     @Value("${local-candle.reconciliation.request-budget-per-pass:10}") private int requestBudget;
  46:     @Value("${analysis.scoring.data-quality.minimum-candles:210}") private int minimumCandles;
  47:     @Value("${local-candle.reconciliation.live-close-grace-seconds:10}") private long liveCloseGraceSeconds;
  48:     @Value("${local-candle.reconciliation.safety-margin-seconds:2}") private long safetyMarginSeconds;
  49: 
  50:     public LocalCandleReconciliationService(JdbcTemplate jdbc, CoinConfigurationService coins,
  51:             BinanceMarketDataProperties properties, BinanceMarketDataClient binance, CandleRepository candles,
  52:             LocalReconciliationAuditRepository audit, CandleAnalysisOutcomeStore outcomes,
  53:             @Qualifier("candleReconciliationExecutor") Executor executor, PlatformTransactionManager manager) {
  54:         this.jdbc=jdbc;this.coins=coins;this.properties=properties;this.binance=binance;this.candles=candles;
  55:         this.audit=audit;this.outcomes=outcomes;this.executor=executor;this.tx=new TransactionTemplate(manager);
  56:     }
  57: 
  58:     @Scheduled(initialDelayString="${local-candle.reconciliation.initial-delay-ms:15000}",fixedDelayString="${local-candle.reconciliation.scan-delay-ms:5000}")
  59:     public void scheduledScan(){ request("SCHEDULE"); }
  60:     public void requestAfterReconnect(long generation){
  61:         Instant local=Instant.now();lastReconnectAdmission=exchangeClock==null?local:exchangeClock.correctJvm(local);
  62:         request("RECONNECT_"+generation);
  63:     }
  64:     public boolean isIdle(){return !running.get();}
  65:     public Map<String,Object> health(){
  66:         Map<String,Object> result=new LinkedHashMap<>();result.put("enabled",enabled);result.put("inFlight",running.get());
  67:         try {
  68:             result.put("pendingCandidates",jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM local_reconciliation_audit WHERE status='PENDING'",Long.class));
  69:             result.put("recoveredCount",jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM local_reconciliation_audit WHERE status='RECOVERED'",Long.class));
  70:             result.put("gapBackfilledCount",jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM local_reconciliation_audit WHERE status='GAP_BACKFILLED'",Long.class));
  71:             result.put("expiredCount",jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM local_reconciliation_audit WHERE status='EXPIRED'",Long.class));
  72:             Instant local=Instant.now();Instant reference=exchangeClock==null?local:exchangeClock.correctJvm(local);
  73:             List<Map<String,Object>> activeGaps=new ArrayList<>();
  74:             for(String symbol:coins.enabledSymbols())for(String interval:properties.getIntervals()){
  75:                 Instant lower=reference.minusSeconds(Math.max(1,minimumCandles)*intervalSeconds(interval));
  76:                 Timestamp first=jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT MIN(open_time) FROM candle WHERE symbol=? AND interval_code=?",Timestamp.class,symbol,interval);
  77:                 if(first==null)continue;
  78:                 Long count=jdbc.queryForObject("""
  79:                     SELECT COUNT(*) FROM local_reconciliation_audit
  80:                     WHERE subject_type='CANDLE_DATA' AND symbol=? AND interval_code=?
  81:                       AND status='EXPIRED' AND failure_reason='NOT_ON_EXCHANGE'
  82:                       AND candle_open_time>=? AND candle_open_time>?
  83:                     """,Long.class,symbol,interval,Timestamp.from(lower),first);
  84:                 if(count!=null&&count>0)activeGaps.add(Map.of("symbol",symbol,"interval",interval,"count",count));
  85:             }
  86:             result.put("exchangeGapBlockedByLane",activeGaps);
  87:             result.put("exchangeGapBlockedCount",activeGaps.stream().mapToLong(row->((Number)row.get("count")).longValue()).sum());
  88:         }catch(RuntimeException unavailable){result.put("status","NOT_MIGRATED_OR_UNAVAILABLE");}
  89:         return result;
  90:     }
  91: 
  92:     private void request(String reason){
  93:         if(!enabled || !"OFF".equals(mode) || !running.compareAndSet(false,true))return;
  94:         try { executor.execute(()-> { try { runPass(reason); } finally { running.set(false); } }); }
  95:         catch(RuntimeException rejected){running.set(false);log.warn("[FIX-153][RECONCILIATION_REJECTED] reason={}",reason,rejected);}
  96:     }
  97: 
  98:     private void runPass(String reason){
  99:         if(drain!=null&&!drain.beginReconciliation())return;
 100:         try {
 101:             // The server-time read happens before every REST kline request and is
 102:             // the sole exchange-time reference for this pass.
 103:             Instant exchangeNow=binance.getServerTime();
 104:             Instant databaseNow=jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6)",Timestamp.class).toInstant();
 105:             int[] remaining={Math.max(0,requestBudget)};
 106:             for(Candidate candidate:openCandidates(exchangeNow,databaseNow)) {
 107:                 if(drain!=null&&drain.draining())return;
 108:                 if(remaining[0]--<=0)break;
 109:                 recover(candidate,exchangeNow,databaseNow,false);
 110:             }
 111:             if(remaining[0]>0) for(Candidate candidate:gapCandidates(exchangeNow,databaseNow)) {
 112:                 if(drain!=null&&drain.draining())return;
 113:                 if(remaining[0]--<=0)break;
 114:                 recover(candidate,exchangeNow,databaseNow,true);
 115:             }
 116:             log.debug("[FIX-153][RECONCILIATION_PASS] reason={}, exchangeNow={}, budget={}",reason,exchangeNow,requestBudget-remaining[0]);
 117:         } catch(RuntimeException failure) {
 118:             log.warn("[FIX-153][RECONCILIATION_PASS_FAILED] reason={}; no candle was treated as recovered",reason,failure);
 119:         } finally {if(drain!=null)drain.completeReconciliation();}
 120:     }
 121: 
 122:     private List<Candidate> openCandidates(Instant exchangeNow,Instant databaseNow){
 123:         List<Candidate> result=new ArrayList<>();
 124:         for(String interval:properties.getIntervals()) {
 125:             long seconds=intervalSeconds(interval), window=Math.max(1,minimumCandles)*seconds;
 126:             Instant lower=exchangeNow.minusSeconds(window);
 127:             Instant eligible=exchangeNow.minusSeconds(liveCloseGraceSeconds+safetyMarginSeconds);
 128:             Instant latestOpen=eligible.minusSeconds(seconds);
 129:             result.addAll(jdbc.query("""
 130:                 SELECT symbol,interval_code,open_time FROM candle
 131:                 WHERE closed=0 AND interval_code=? AND open_time>=? AND open_time<=?
 132:                   AND (ingestion_source IS NULL OR ingestion_source<>'BACKFILL')
 133:                 ORDER BY open_time ASC LIMIT 50
 134:                 """,(rs,n)->{
 135:                     Instant open=rs.getTimestamp(3).toInstant();
 136:                     return new Candidate(rs.getString(1),rs.getString(2),open,expectedClose(open,rs.getString(2)));
 137:                 },interval,Timestamp.from(lower),Timestamp.from(latestOpen)));
 138:         }
 139:         return result.stream().filter(c->audit.due(c.symbol,c.interval,c.openTime,databaseNow))
 140:             .filter(this::positiveLossEvidence).toList();
 141:     }
 142: 
 143:     /** Read-only discovery of absent expected identities in the active quality window. */
 144:     private List<Candidate> gapCandidates(Instant exchangeNow,Instant databaseNow){
 145:         List<Candidate> result=new ArrayList<>();
 146:         for(String symbol:coins.enabledSymbols()) for(String interval:properties.getIntervals()) {
 147:             long seconds=intervalSeconds(interval), window=Math.max(1,minimumCandles)*seconds;
 148:             Instant eligible=exchangeNow.minusSeconds(liveCloseGraceSeconds+safetyMarginSeconds);
 149:             Instant first=Instant.ofEpochSecond((exchangeNow.minusSeconds(window).getEpochSecond()/seconds)*seconds);
 150:             Set<Instant> present=new HashSet<>(jdbc.query("""
 151:                 SELECT open_time FROM candle WHERE symbol=? AND interval_code=? AND open_time>=? AND open_time<?
 152:                 """,(rs,n)->rs.getTimestamp(1).toInstant(),symbol,interval,Timestamp.from(first),Timestamp.from(eligible)));
 153:             for(Instant open=first;open.plusSeconds(seconds).compareTo(eligible)<=0;open=open.plusSeconds(seconds)) {
 154:                 if(!present.contains(open) && audit.due(symbol,interval,open,databaseNow))
 155:                     result.add(new Candidate(symbol,interval,open,open.plusSeconds(seconds).minusMillis(1)));
 156:             }
 157:         }
 158:         return result;
 159:     }
 160: 
 161:     private void recover(Candidate candidate,Instant exchangeNow,Instant databaseNow,boolean gap) {
 162:         var prior=audit.findCandle(candidate.symbol,candidate.interval,candidate.openTime);
 163:         int attempts=prior.map(s->s.attempts()+1).orElse(1);
 164:         try {
 165:             List<BinanceKline> rows=binance.getKlines(candidate.symbol,candidate.interval,candidate.openTime,candidate.closeTime);
 166:             List<BinanceKline> exact=rows.stream().filter(k->k.openTime().equals(candidate.openTime)&&k.closeTime().equals(candidate.closeTime)).toList();
 167:             if(rows.isEmpty() && gap) {
 168:                 List<BinanceKline> first=binance.getKlinesAtOrAfter(candidate.symbol,candidate.interval,candidate.openTime);
 169:                 if(!first.isEmpty() && first.getFirst().openTime().isAfter(candidate.openTime)) {
 170:                     terminalUnavailable(candidate,exchangeNow,"NOT_ON_EXCHANGE");return;
 171:                 }
 172:                 pending(candidate,attempts,databaseNow,"REST_EMPTY",exchangeNow);return;
 173:             }
 174:             if(rows.isEmpty()) { pending(candidate,attempts,databaseNow,"REST_EMPTY",exchangeNow);return; }
 175:             if(candidate.closeTime.compareTo(exchangeNow)>=0) {
 176:                 pending(candidate,attempts,databaseNow,"REST_NOT_FINAL",exchangeNow);return;
 177:             }
 178:             if(exact.size()!=1) {
 179:                 pending(candidate,attempts,databaseNow,"REST_MISMATCH",exchangeNow);return;
 180:             }
 181:             BinanceKline k=exact.getFirst();
 182:             tx.executeWithoutResult(status->{
 183:                 boolean changed=gap ? candles.insertAuthoritativeGapBackfill(candidate.symbol,candidate.interval,candidate.openTime,k.closeTime(),
 184:                     k.openPrice(),k.highPrice(),k.lowPrice(),k.closePrice(),k.volume(),k.quoteAssetVolume(),k.numberOfTrades(),k.takerBuyBaseVolume(),k.takerBuyQuoteVolume())
 185:                     : candles.finalizeExistingOpenFromAuthoritative(candidate.symbol,candidate.interval,candidate.openTime,k.closeTime(),
 186:                     k.openPrice(),k.highPrice(),k.lowPrice(),k.closePrice(),k.volume(),k.quoteAssetVolume(),k.numberOfTrades(),k.takerBuyBaseVolume(),k.takerBuyQuoteVolume());
 187:                 String result=changed ? (gap?"GAP_BACKFILLED":"RECOVERED") : (gap?"ALREADY_PRESENT":"ALREADY_CLOSED");
 188:                 audit.terminal(candidate.symbol,candidate.interval,candidate.openTime,result,null,exchangeNow,k.openTime(),k.closeTime());
 189:                 if(changed) outcomes.record(candidate.symbol,candidate.interval,candidate.openTime,"SKIPPED_NOT_LIVE",result,null,gap?"AUTHORITATIVE_GAP_BACKFILL":"AUTHORITATIVE_RECOVERY");
 190:             });
 191:         } catch(RuntimeException failure) { pending(candidate,attempts,databaseNow,"REST_FAILURE",exchangeNow); }
 192:     }
 193: 
 194:     private void pending(Candidate c,int attempts,Instant databaseNow,String reason,Instant exchangeNow){
 195:         long delay=Math.min(300,attempts==1?5:attempts==2?15:60L*(1L<<Math.min(2,attempts-3)));
 196:         audit.pending(c.symbol,c.interval,c.openTime,attempts,databaseNow.plusSeconds(delay),reason,exchangeNow);
 197:     }
 198:     private boolean positiveLossEvidence(Candidate candidate){
 199:         // This relies on the current FIX-131 invariant: one primary WebSocket
 200:         // session invokes processKline synchronously and in Binance receive
 201:         // order. If streams are sharded or processing becomes asynchronous,
 202:         // this successor proof must be redesigned and re-tested.
 203:         Instant successor=candidate.openTime.plusSeconds(intervalSeconds(candidate.interval));
 204:         Integer successorPresent=jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM candle WHERE symbol=? AND interval_code=? AND open_time=?",Integer.class,
 205:             candidate.symbol,candidate.interval,Timestamp.from(successor));
 206:         return successorPresent!=null&&successorPresent>0 || (lastReconnectAdmission!=null && !lastReconnectAdmission.isBefore(candidate.closeTime));
 207:     }
 208:     private void terminalUnavailable(Candidate candidate,Instant exchangeNow,String reason){
 209:         tx.executeWithoutResult(status->{
 210:             audit.terminal(candidate.symbol,candidate.interval,candidate.openTime,"EXPIRED",reason,exchangeNow,null,null);
 211:             outcomes.record(candidate.symbol,candidate.interval,candidate.openTime,"SKIPPED_DATA_UNAVAILABLE",reason,null,"AUTHORITATIVE_RECONCILIATION");
 212:         });
 213:     }
 214:     private static long intervalSeconds(String interval){return switch(interval){case "1m"->60;case "5m"->300;case "15m"->900;case "1h"->3600;case "4h"->14400;case "1d"->86400;default->throw new IllegalArgumentException("Unsupported interval: "+interval);};}
 215:     private static Instant expectedClose(Instant open,String interval){return open.plusSeconds(intervalSeconds(interval)).minusMillis(1);}
 216:     private record Candidate(String symbol,String interval,Instant openTime,Instant closeTime) {}
 217: }

--- src/main/java/com/crypto/local/LocalClosedCandleDetector.java
   1: package com.crypto.local;
   2: 
   3: import com.crypto.administration.service.CoinConfigurationService;
   4: import com.crypto.indicator.event.*;
   5: import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
   6: import org.springframework.scheduling.annotation.Scheduled;
   7: import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Value;
   8: import org.springframework.jdbc.core.JdbcTemplate;
   9: import org.slf4j.*;
  10: import java.time.*;
  11: import java.sql.Timestamp;
  12: import java.util.*;
  13: 
  14: /** FIX-153: local durable candles close the commit-to-memory-dispatch crash window.
  15:  * BACKFILL is never admitted. Legacy NULL provenance is eligible only when the
  16:  * live stream wrote a close-transition token.
  17:  * Small lookback and one admission per idle lane keep detector below live work. */
  18: @Component
  19: public class LocalClosedCandleDetector {
  20:     private static final Logger log=LoggerFactory.getLogger(LocalClosedCandleDetector.class);
  21:     private final JdbcTemplate jdbc;private final CoinConfigurationService coins;
  22:     private final CandleAnalysisDispatcher dispatcher;private final CandleAnalysisExecutionCoordinator coordination;
  23:     private final com.crypto.client.config.binance.BinanceMarketDataProperties properties;
  24:     @Value("${shared-market.mode:OFF}") private String mode;
  25:     @org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired(required=false) private ExchangeClockSynchronizer exchangeClock;
  26:     @org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired(required=false) private CandleAnalysisOutcomeStore outcomes;
  27:     private int nextLane;
  28:     private final Map<String,Retry> retries=new HashMap<>();
  29:     private final Map<String,Instant> analysedUntil=new HashMap<>();
  30:     public LocalClosedCandleDetector(JdbcTemplate jdbc,CoinConfigurationService coins,CandleAnalysisDispatcher dispatcher,CandleAnalysisExecutionCoordinator coordination,com.crypto.client.config.binance.BinanceMarketDataProperties properties){
  31:         this.jdbc=new JdbcTemplate(Objects.requireNonNull(jdbc.getDataSource()));this.jdbc.setQueryTimeout(2);
  32:         this.coins=coins;this.dispatcher=dispatcher;this.coordination=coordination;this.properties=properties;
  33:     }
  34:     @Scheduled(initialDelay=5000,fixedDelayString="${local-candle.detector-delay-ms:2000}")
  35:     public void detect(){
  36:         if(!"OFF".equals(mode))return;
  37:         Instant localNow=Instant.now();Instant now=exchangeClock==null?localNow:exchangeClock.correctJvm(localNow);long deadline=System.nanoTime()+java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit.SECONDS.toNanos(2);
  38:         try {
  39:             var symbols=coins.enabledSymbols();var intervals=properties.getIntervals();
  40:             int total=symbols.size()*intervals.size();
  41:             for(int checked=0;checked<total;checked++){
  42:                 if(System.nanoTime()>deadline)return;
  43:                 // FIX-153: rotate across bounded passes so a slow first lane cannot starve later symbols.
  44:                 int lane=Math.floorMod(nextLane++,total);
  45:                 String symbol=symbols.get(lane/intervals.size()),interval=intervals.get(lane%intervals.size());
  46:                 if(!dispatcher.idle(symbol,interval))continue;
  47:                 long lookback=Math.max(180,com.crypto.shared.SharedEventPolicy.closeGraceSeconds(interval)*2L);
  48:                 // Index is on open_time: include one full interval before the close-age lookback.
  49:                 Instant lowerOpen=now.minusSeconds(intervalSeconds(interval)+lookback);
  50:                 var candidates=jdbc.query("SELECT open_time,close_time FROM candle WHERE symbol=? AND interval_code=? AND closed=1 AND close_transition_token IS NOT NULL AND (ingestion_source IS NULL OR ingestion_source<>'BACKFILL') AND open_time>=? AND close_time<=? ORDER BY open_time ASC LIMIT 4",
  51:                     (rs,n)->new CandleClosedEvent(symbol,interval,rs.getTimestamp(1).toInstant(),rs.getTimestamp(2).toInstant()),symbol,interval,Timestamp.from(lowerOpen),Timestamp.from(now));
  52:                 for(var event:candidates){
  53:                     String retryKey=key(event);
  54:                     Instant knownAnalysedUntil=analysedUntil.get(retryKey);
  55:                     if(knownAnalysedUntil!=null && knownAnalysedUntil.isAfter(now))continue;
  56:                     Retry retry=retries.get(retryKey);
  57:                     if(retry!=null && retry.nextAttempt().isAfter(now))continue;
  58:                     // A healthy analysis is terminal for detector purposes. Do this
  59:                     // before dispatcher admission so ordinary completed candles never
  60:                     // become retry/expiry noise.
  61:                     if(!missing(event)){retries.remove(retryKey);analysedUntil.put(retryKey,eligibilityEndsAfter(event,lookback));continue;}
  62:                     // No database fence; existing striped candle lock covers only check+handoff.
  63:                     var acquired=coordination.tryLock(symbol,interval,event.openTime());
  64:                     if(acquired.isEmpty())continue;
  65:                     try(var ignored=acquired.get()){
  66:                         if(dispatcher.submitDetected(event,()->missing(event))){
  67:                             // Admission is not completion. Retain bounded state until
  68:                             // durable signal/work evidence appears on a later pass.
  69:                             // Every re-admission consumes the same bounded retry budget;
  70:                             // a repeatedly failing worker must not execute forever.
  71:                             recordAdmission(retryKey,event,now,lookback);
  72:                             break;
  73:                         }
  74:                         defer(retryKey,event,now,"NOT_ADMITTED");
  75:                     }
  76:                 }
  77:             }
  78:             for(var iterator=retries.entrySet().iterator();iterator.hasNext();){
  79:                 var entry=iterator.next();
  80:                 // Retention is strictly after the SQL lower-open-time boundary.
  81:                 // Therefore pruning cannot make a still-detectable candle restart
  82:                 // with a fresh retry budget.
  83:                 if(entry.getValue().retainUntil().isBefore(now)){
  84:                     log.info("[FIX-153][DETECTOR_RETRY_PRUNED] candle={}, attempts={}, reason=NO_LONGER_ELIGIBLE",entry.getKey(),entry.getValue().attempts());
  85:                     iterator.remove();
  86:                 }
  87:             }
  88:             analysedUntil.entrySet().removeIf(e->!e.getValue().isAfter(now));
  89:         }catch(RuntimeException failure){LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.count(LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.Event.DETECTOR_FAILED);
  90:             log.warn("[FIX-153][DETECTOR_FAILED] no checkpoint advanced; next bounded pass may retry",failure);}
  91:     }
  92:     private static String key(CandleClosedEvent e){return e.symbol()+"|"+e.intervalCode()+"|"+e.openTime();}
  93:     private void defer(String key,CandleClosedEvent event,Instant now,String reason){
  94:         Retry prior=retries.get(key);int attempts=prior==null?1:prior.attempts()+1;
  95:         long delay=Math.min(60,1L<<Math.min(6,attempts));
  96:         long lookback=Math.max(180,com.crypto.shared.SharedEventPolicy.closeGraceSeconds(event.intervalCode())*2L);
  97:         Instant retryDeadline=event.closeTime().plusSeconds(lookback);
  98:         Instant retainUntil=eligibilityEndsAfter(event,lookback);
  99:         if(attempts>6 || !retryDeadline.isAfter(now)){
 100:             // Keep a terminal marker until this candle has aged out of the SQL
 101:             // lookback. Removing it immediately would restart the retry budget.
 102:             retries.put(key,new Retry(attempts,Instant.MAX,retryDeadline,retainUntil));
 103:             recordExpired(event,reason);
 104:             log.warn("[FIX-153][DETECTOR_GAP_EXPIRED] candle={}, attempts={}, reason={}",key,attempts,reason);return;
 105:         }
 106:         retries.put(key,new Retry(attempts,now.plusSeconds(delay),retryDeadline,retainUntil));
 107:         log.info("[FIX-153][DETECTOR_RETRY_DEFERRED] candle={}, attempts={}, nextUtc={}, reason={}",key,attempts,now.plusSeconds(delay),reason);
 108:     }
 109:     private void recordAdmission(String key,CandleClosedEvent event,Instant now,long lookback){
 110:         Retry prior=retries.get(key);int attempts=prior==null?1:prior.attempts()+1;
 111:         Instant retryDeadline=event.closeTime().plusSeconds(lookback);
 112:         Instant retainUntil=eligibilityEndsAfter(event,lookback);
 113:         if(attempts>6 || !retryDeadline.isAfter(now)){
 114:             // See defer(): this marker is deliberately retained until the query
 115:             // can no longer rediscover this candle.
 116:             retries.put(key,new Retry(attempts,Instant.MAX,retryDeadline,retainUntil));
 117:             recordExpired(event,"ADMISSION_RETRY_LIMIT");
 118:             log.warn("[FIX-153][DETECTOR_GAP_EXPIRED] candle={}, attempts={}, reason=ADMISSION_RETRY_LIMIT",key,attempts);
 119:             return;
 120:         }
 121:         long delay=retryDelaySeconds(attempts);
 122:         retries.put(key,new Retry(attempts,now.plusSeconds(delay),retryDeadline,retainUntil));
 123:         log.info("[FIX-153][DETECTOR_ADMITTED_AWAITING_EVIDENCE] candle={}, attempts={}, nextUtc={}",key,attempts,now.plusSeconds(delay));
 124:     }
 125:     /** Strictly after the candidate predicate: open_time >= now - interval - lookback. */
 126:     private static Instant eligibilityEndsAfter(CandleClosedEvent event,long lookback){
 127:         return event.openTime().plusSeconds(intervalSeconds(event.intervalCode())+lookback+1);
 128:     }
 129:     private static long retryDelaySeconds(int attempts){return Math.min(60,1L<<Math.min(6,attempts));}
 130:     private record Retry(int attempts,Instant nextAttempt,Instant retryDeadline,Instant retainUntil) {}
 131:     private static long intervalSeconds(String interval){
 132:         return switch(interval){case "1m"->60;case "5m"->300;case "15m"->900;case "1h"->3600;case "4h"->14400;case "1d"->86400;
 133:             default->throw new IllegalArgumentException("FIX-153 unsupported detector interval: "+interval);};
 134:     }
 135:     private boolean missing(CandleClosedEvent e){
 136:         if(outcomes!=null && outcomes.exists(e.symbol(),e.intervalCode(),e.openTime()))return false;
 137:         return jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT (EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM trade_signal WHERE symbol=? AND interval_code=? AND candle_open_time=?) OR EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM signal_processing_work WHERE symbol=? AND interval_code=? AND candle_open_time=?))",Integer.class,
 138:             e.symbol(),e.intervalCode(),Timestamp.from(e.openTime()),e.symbol(),e.intervalCode(),Timestamp.from(e.openTime()))==0;
 139:     }
 140:     private void recordExpired(CandleClosedEvent event,String reason){
 141:         if(outcomes!=null)outcomes.record(event.symbol(),event.intervalCode(),event.openTime(),"ANALYSIS_EXPIRED",reason,null,"LOCAL_DETECTOR");
 142:     }
 143: }

--- src/main/java/com/crypto/indicator/event/CandleAnalysisDispatcher.java
   1: package com.crypto.indicator.event;
   2: 
   3: import jakarta.annotation.PreDestroy;
   4: import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Qualifier;
   5: import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
   6: import org.slf4j.Logger;
   7: import org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;
   8: import com.crypto.local.LocalRuntimeDiagnostics;
   9: import static com.crypto.local.LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.Event.*;
  10: import java.time.Instant;
  11: import java.util.*;
  12: import java.util.concurrent.*;
  13: 
  14: /** FIX-153: one running task per lane. 1m keeps latest pending; higher TFs retain
  15:  * a bounded FIFO. Reservations and admission share the lane monitor, including
  16:  * detector idle check. No analysis executes on ingestion threads at saturation. */
  17: @Component
  18: public class CandleAnalysisDispatcher {
  19:     private static final Logger log=LoggerFactory.getLogger(CandleAnalysisDispatcher.class);
  20:     private final Executor executor;
  21:     private final CandleClosedAnalysisWorker worker;
  22:     private final Map<String,Lane> lanes=new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
  23:     private volatile boolean stopped;
  24:     public CandleAnalysisDispatcher(@Qualifier("candleAnalysisExecutor") Executor executor,CandleClosedAnalysisWorker worker){this.executor=executor;this.worker=worker;}
  25:     public void submit(CandleClosedEvent event){admit(event,false,()->true);}
  26:     /** Check exact candle evidence under the detector's existing in-process lock.
  27:      * Check+reserve+enqueue is atomic with live admission; detector never replaces pending live work. */
  28:     public boolean submitDetected(CandleClosedEvent event,java.util.function.BooleanSupplier missing){return admit(event,true,missing);}
  29:     public boolean idle(String symbol,String interval){Lane l=lanes.get(symbol+"|"+interval);if(l==null)return !stopped;synchronized(l){return !stopped&&l.active==null&&l.pending.isEmpty();}}
  30:     /** A detector reservation is in-flight admission work, even before a task exists. */
  31:     public boolean idleAll(){return !stopped && lanes.values().stream().allMatch(l->{synchronized(l){return l.active==null&&l.pending.isEmpty()&&l.reserved.isEmpty()&&l.deferredLive.isEmpty();}});}
  32:     private boolean admit(CandleClosedEvent e,boolean detector,java.util.function.BooleanSupplier missing){
  33:         if(e==null || stopped)return false;
  34:         String key=e.symbol().trim().toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT)+"|"+e.intervalCode();
  35:         Lane lane=lanes.computeIfAbsent(key,k->new Lane());
  36:         if(detector){
  37:             synchronized(lane){
  38:                 if(stopped || lane.active!=null || !lane.pending.isEmpty() || !lane.reserved.add(e.openTime())) return false;
  39:             }
  40:             // FIX-153: reserve first, but never hold admission's monitor across database I/O.
  41:             boolean absent;
  42:             try {absent=missing.getAsBoolean();}
  43:             catch(RuntimeException failure){
  44:                 CandleClosedEvent handoff;
  45:                 synchronized(lane){
  46:                     handoff=lane.deferredLive.remove(e.openTime());
  47:                     if(handoff!=null)enqueueReserved(lane,key,handoff,false);
  48:                     else lane.reserved.remove(e.openTime());
  49:                 }
  50:                 throw failure;
  51:             }
  52:             synchronized(lane){
  53:                 // A committed live close that arrived during the detector's database
  54:                 // check owns admission. Transfer it to the queue while retaining
  55:                 // its reservation and the lane monitor: no second SQL check and no
  56:                 // interleaving admission window can lose it.
  57:                 CandleClosedEvent liveHandoff=lane.deferredLive.remove(e.openTime());
  58:                 if(liveHandoff!=null)return enqueueReserved(lane,key,liveHandoff,false);
  59:                 if(!absent || stopped || lane.active!=null || !lane.pending.isEmpty()){
  60:                     lane.reserved.remove(e.openTime());return false;
  61:                 }
  62:             }
  63:         }
  64:         synchronized(lane){
  65:             if(stopped || (detector && (lane.active!=null || !lane.pending.isEmpty()))){if(detector)lane.reserved.remove(e.openTime());return false;}
  66:             if(!detector && lane.reserved.contains(e.openTime())){
  67:                 // Detector owns the temporary reservation only. Preserve the exact
  68:                 // committed event and let the detector hand it straight back once
  69:                 // its read completes; no live close is discarded in this window.
  70:                 lane.deferredLive.putIfAbsent(e.openTime(),e);return true;
  71:             }
  72:             if(!detector && !lane.reserved.add(e.openTime())){LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.count(DUPLICATE_ADMISSION);return false;}
  73:             Task task=new Task(e,detector);
  74:             if("1m".equals(e.intervalCode()) && !lane.pending.isEmpty()){
  75:                 Task previous=lane.pending.peekLast();
  76:                 // A delayed older callback cannot replace newer pending work.
  77:                 if(!e.openTime().isAfter(previous.event.openTime())){lane.reserved.remove(e.openTime());LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.count(OVERFLOW);return false;}
  78:                 previous=lane.pending.removeLast();lane.reserved.remove(previous.event.openTime());lane.deferredLive.remove(previous.event.openTime());LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.count(REPLACED);
  79:                 log.info("[FIX-153][PENDING_REPLACED] lane={}, skippedOpenUtc={}, keptOpenUtc={}, businessExecuted=false",key,previous.event.openTime(),e.openTime());
  80:             }else if(lane.pending.size()>=4){lane.reserved.remove(e.openTime());LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.count(OVERFLOW);
  81:                 log.info("[FIX-153][LANE_OVERFLOW] lane={}, rejectedOpenUtc={}, pending={}, policy=BOUNDED_FIFO",key,e.openTime(),lane.pending.size());return false;}
  82:             lane.pending.add(task);LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.count(ADMITTED);
  83:             if(detector)LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.count(DETECTOR_ADMITTED);
  84:             return schedule(lane,key);
  85:         }
  86:     }
  87:     /** Caller holds lane's monitor and has already retained e.openTime() in reserved. */
  88:     private boolean enqueueReserved(Lane lane,String key,CandleClosedEvent e,boolean detector){
  89:         if(stopped){lane.reserved.remove(e.openTime());return false;}
  90:         Task task=new Task(e,detector);
  91:         if("1m".equals(e.intervalCode()) && !lane.pending.isEmpty()){
  92:             Task previous=lane.pending.peekLast();
  93:             if(!e.openTime().isAfter(previous.event.openTime())){lane.reserved.remove(e.openTime());LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.count(OVERFLOW);return false;}
  94:             previous=lane.pending.removeLast();lane.reserved.remove(previous.event.openTime());lane.deferredLive.remove(previous.event.openTime());LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.count(REPLACED);
  95:             log.info("[FIX-153][PENDING_REPLACED] lane={}, skippedOpenUtc={}, keptOpenUtc={}, businessExecuted=false",key,previous.event.openTime(),e.openTime());
  96:         }else if(lane.pending.size()>=4){lane.reserved.remove(e.openTime());LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.count(OVERFLOW);
  97:             log.info("[FIX-153][LANE_OVERFLOW] lane={}, rejectedOpenUtc={}, pending={}, policy=BOUNDED_FIFO",key,e.openTime(),lane.pending.size());return false;}
  98:         lane.pending.add(task);LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.count(ADMITTED);
  99:         if(detector)LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.count(DETECTOR_ADMITTED);
 100:         return schedule(lane,key);
 101:     }
 102:     private boolean schedule(Lane lane,String key){
 103:         if(stopped)return false;
 104:         if(lane.active!=null)return true;
 105:         Task task=lane.pending.pollFirst();if(task==null)return true;lane.active=task;
 106:         try {executor.execute(()->{
 107:             synchronized(lane){if(stopped || lane.active!=task)return;}
 108:             long start=System.nanoTime();LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.count(ANALYSIS_ATTEMPTED);LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.analysis(key,Instant.now());
 109:             String outcome="THREW_OR_UNCONFIRMED";
 110:             try {outcome=worker.processShared(task.event,task.event.closeTime(),true);}
 111:             catch(RuntimeException failure){log.error("[FIX-153][ANALYSIS_TASK_FAILED] lane={}, openUtc={}",key,task.event.openTime(),failure);}
 112:             finally {
 113:                 LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.count(ANALYSIS_FINISHED);
 114:                 log.debug("[FIX-153][ANALYSIS_TASK_RETURNED] lane={}, openUtc={}, outcome={}, callbackMs={}; task return alone is not wallet commit evidence",key,task.event.openTime(),outcome,(System.nanoTime()-start)/1_000_000);
 115:                 synchronized(lane){lane.reserved.remove(task.event.openTime());lane.deferredLive.remove(task.event.openTime());lane.active=null;schedule(lane,key);}
 116:             }
 117:         });return true;}catch(RejectedExecutionException failure){
 118:             // Release every queued reservation on rejection: local detector may reconsider gaps.
 119:             LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.count(SUBMISSION_REJECTED);lane.active=null;lane.reserved.remove(task.event.openTime());
 120:             lane.deferredLive.remove(task.event.openTime());
 121:             for(Task queued:lane.pending){lane.reserved.remove(queued.event.openTime());lane.deferredLive.remove(queued.event.openTime());}lane.pending.clear();
 122:             log.info("[FIX-153][EXECUTOR_REJECTED] lane={}, openUtc={}, ranOnIngestionThread=false",key,task.event.openTime());
 123:             return false;
 124:         }
 125:     }
 126:     public Map<String,Object> snapshot(){Map<String,Object> result=new TreeMap<>();lanes.forEach((k,l)->{synchronized(l){result.put(k,Map.of("active",l.active!=null,"pending",l.pending.size(),"reservations",l.reserved.size()));}});return result;}
 127:     @PreDestroy public void stop(){stopped=true;lanes.values().forEach(l->{synchronized(l){l.pending.clear();l.reserved.clear();l.deferredLive.clear();}});}
 128:     private static class Lane {final Deque<Task> pending=new ArrayDeque<>();final Set<Instant> reserved=new HashSet<>();final Map<Instant,CandleClosedEvent> deferredLive=new HashMap<>();Task active;}
 129:     private record Task(CandleClosedEvent event,boolean detector) {}
 130: }

  60:     private java.time.Instant referenceNow(){
  61:         java.time.Instant local=java.time.Instant.now();
  62:         return exchangeClock==null?local:exchangeClock.correctJvm(local);
  63:     }
  64: 
  65:     // FIX-153: use persisted close authority; a queued candle can expire before analysis starts.
  66:     public void process(CandleClosedEvent event) { processShared(event,event.closeTime(),true); }

  69:      * analysis may persist evidence but never registers or invokes wallet work. */
  70:     public String processShared(CandleClosedEvent event, java.time.Instant closeTime, boolean liveSource) {
  71:         return processShared(event,closeTime,liveSource,null);

  73: 
  74:     /** FIX-132 event ownership is explicit, including across executor boundaries. */
  75:     public String processShared(CandleClosedEvent event, java.time.Instant closeTime, boolean liveSource, Long sourceEventId) {
  76:         // FIX-137: one correlated boundary around every legacy/shared worker attempt.
  77:         long started = System.nanoTime();
  78:         String outcome = "UNCONFIRMED";
  79:         log.info("[FIX-137][WORKER_START] event={}, symbol={}, interval={}, open={}, close={}, liveSource={}",
  80:                 sourceEventId,event.symbol(),event.intervalCode(),event.openTime(),closeTime,liveSource);
  81:         try {
  82:             outcome = processSharedTraced(event,closeTime,liveSource,sourceEventId,started);
  83:             return outcome;
  84:         } finally {
  85:             log.info("[FIX-137][WORKER_END] event={}, symbol={}, interval={}, open={}, outcome={}, elapsedMs={}",
  86:                     sourceEventId,event.symbol(),event.intervalCode(),event.openTime(),outcome,
  87:                     (System.nanoTime()-started)/1_000_000);
  88:         }
  89:     }
  90: 
  91:     private String processSharedTraced(CandleClosedEvent event, java.time.Instant closeTime,
  92:             boolean liveSource, Long sourceEventId, long started) {
  93:         // FIX-140: only explicitly attributed safe retries can resume an existing signal.
  94:         // Generic REVIEW_REQUIRED rows and natural-key matches never authorize execution.
  95:         if(sourceEventId!=null && liveSource) {
  96:             var retry=sharedWorkEvidence.queryForList("SELECT signal_id FROM signal_processing_work WHERE source_event_id=? AND status='SYMBOL_LOCK_RETRY' AND failure_stage='INITIAL_SYMBOL_LOCK_ROLLED_BACK' AND next_attempt_at<=CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6)",sourceEventId);
  97:             if(retry.size()==1) {
  98:                 TradeSignal saved=tradeSignalRepository.findById(((Number)retry.getFirst().get("signal_id")).longValue()).orElseThrow();
  99:                 if(!event.symbol().equals(saved.getSymbol()) || !event.intervalCode().equals(saved.getInterval())
 100:                     || !event.openTime().equals(saved.getCandleOpenTime()))return "REVIEW_REQUIRED";
 101:                 try {paperTradingService.processSharedSignal(saved,sourceEventId);}
 102:                 catch(com.crypto.execution.processing.SymbolLockRetryScheduled scheduled) {return processingOutcome(sourceEventId);}
 103:                 catch(Exception failure) {log.error("[FIX-140][RETRY_FAILED] event={}",sourceEventId,failure);return "REVIEW_REQUIRED";}
 104:                 return processingOutcome(sourceEventId);
 105:             }
 106:         }
 107:         String stage = "COORDINATION_LOCK";
 108:         traceStage(sourceEventId,event,stage,started);
 109:         // FIX-074: serialize only the exact candle identity against FIX-043 recovery.
 110:         // This removes the duplicate trade_signal race while preserving parallelism across
 111:         // independent symbols/timeframes and does not alter scoring or execution semantics.
 112:         try (CandleAnalysisExecutionCoordinator.LockHandle ignored = executionCoordinator.lock(
 113:                 event.symbol(), event.intervalCode(), event.openTime())) {
 114:             log.info("Processing committed CandleClosedEvent: symbol={}, interval={}, openTime={}",
 115:                     event.symbol(), event.intervalCode(), event.openTime());
 116: 
 117:             stage = "DATA_QUALITY";
 118:             traceStage(sourceEventId,event,stage,started);
 119:             CandleDataQualityResult dataQuality = candleDataQualityService.validate(
 120:                     event.symbol(), event.intervalCode());
 121:             if (!dataQuality.valid()) {
 122:                 log.warn(
 123:                         "Automatic analysis blocked by candle data quality: symbol={}, interval={}, warnings={}",
 124:                         event.symbol(), event.intervalCode(), dataQuality.warnings());
 125:                 return terminal(event,"SKIPPED_DATA_UNAVAILABLE","DATA_QUALITY_BLOCKED","DATA_QUALITY_BLOCKED");
 126:             }
 127: 
 128:             stage = "INDICATORS";
 129:             traceStage(sourceEventId,event,stage,started);
 130:             Optional<TechnicalIndicator> indicatorResult = technicalIndicatorService.calculateAndPersist(
 131:                     event.symbol(), event.intervalCode(), event.openTime());
 132:             if (indicatorResult.isEmpty()) {
 133:                 log.info(
 134:                         "Automatic analysis skipped: symbol={}, interval={}, openTime={}, reason=history/as-of candle unavailable",
 135:                         event.symbol(), event.intervalCode(), event.openTime());
 136:                 return terminal(event,"SKIPPED_DATA_UNAVAILABLE","HISTORY_UNAVAILABLE","HISTORY_UNAVAILABLE");
 137:             }
 138: 
 139:             TechnicalIndicator indicator = indicatorResult.get();
 140:             stage = "EXISTING_SIGNAL_CHECK";
 141:             traceStage(sourceEventId,event,stage,started);
 142:             if (tradeSignalRepository.existsBySymbolAndIntervalAndCandleOpenTime(
 143:                     indicator.getSymbol(), indicator.getIntervalCode(), indicator.getCandleOpenTime())) {
 144:                 log.info(
 145:                         "Automatic analysis skipped: signal already exists for symbol={}, interval={}, candleOpenTime={}",
 146:                         indicator.getSymbol(), indicator.getIntervalCode(), indicator.getCandleOpenTime());
 147:                 if(sourceEventId==null)return terminal(event,"SKIPPED_ALREADY_ANALYSED","SIGNAL_ALREADY_EXISTS","ALREADY_EXISTS");
 148:                 // FIX-138: an expired source is historical even when originally LIVE.
 149:                 // Never register/replay work for an already existing historical signal.
 150:                 if(!liveSource || (closeTime!=null && !com.crypto.shared.SharedEventPolicy.closeEligible(
 151:                         event.intervalCode(),closeTime,referenceNow())))return terminal(event,"SKIPPED_ALREADY_ANALYSED","HISTORICAL_SIGNAL_EXISTS","HISTORICAL_ALREADY_EXISTS");
 152:                 // Existing signal is NOT processing completion. Only an attributed,
 153:                 // completed work row establishes a prior shared business outcome.
 154:                 Integer completed=sharedWorkEvidence.queryForObject("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM signal_processing_work w JOIN shared_market_event_delivery d ON d.source_event_id=w.source_event_id WHERE w.symbol=? AND w.interval_code=? AND w.candle_open_time=? AND w.status='COMPLETED' AND d.symbol=w.symbol AND d.interval_code=w.interval_code AND d.candle_open_time=w.candle_open_time",Integer.class,event.symbol(),event.intervalCode(),java.sql.Timestamp.from(event.openTime()));
 155:                 if(completed==0) log.warn("[FIX-137][EXISTING_SIGNAL_REVIEW] event={}, symbol={}, interval={}, open={}, reason=NO_MATCHING_COMPLETED_SHARED_WORK; this does not prove work is absent",
 156:                         sourceEventId,event.symbol(),event.intervalCode(),event.openTime());
 157:                 return completed>0?"ALREADY_COMPLETED":"REVIEW_REQUIRED";
 158:             }

 166:             }
 167:             stage = "LIVE_SIGNAL_AND_REGISTRATION";
 168:             traceStage(sourceEventId,event,stage,started);

 170:                 ? analysisService.analyzeForProcessing(indicator, com.crypto.execution.processing.ProcessingOrigin.WORKER)
 171:                 : analysisService.analyzeForProcessing(indicator, com.crypto.execution.processing.ProcessingOrigin.WORKER,sourceEventId);

 173:             stage = "SIGNAL_PROCESSING";

 175:             Optional<PaperPosition> position = sourceEventId == null ? paperTradingService.processSignal(signal)
 176:                     : paperTradingService.processSharedSignal(signal,sourceEventId);
 177: 
 178:             log.info(

 180:                     indicator.getSymbol(), indicator.getIntervalCode(), indicator.getCandleOpenTime(),

 182:             // FIX-153: Optional.empty is not a completed/expired execution acknowledgment.
 183:             return sourceEventId==null?localProcessingOutcome(signal.getId()):processingOutcome(sourceEventId);
 184:         } catch (com.crypto.execution.processing.SymbolLockRetryScheduled scheduled) {
 185:             return sourceEventId==null?"REVIEW_REQUIRED":processingOutcome(sourceEventId);
 186:         } catch (Exception exception) {
 187:             // FIX-043: never let one analysis failure kill the dispatcher lane. The next candle must
 188:             // continue. FIX-132 LIVE persists this failure for reconciliation; legacy
 189:             // recovery must not independently execute an uncertain shared outcome.
 190:             log.error("[FIX-137][WORKER_STAGE_FAILED] event={}, symbol={}, interval={}, open={}, stage={}, elapsedMs={}",
 191:                     sourceEventId,event.symbol(),event.intervalCode(),event.openTime(),stage,
 192:                     (System.nanoTime()-started)/1_000_000,exception);
 193:             log.error("Automatic candle flow failed for {} {} at {}",
 194:                     event.symbol(), event.intervalCode(), event.openTime(), exception);
 195:             if(sourceEventId != null) {
 196:                 try {
 197:                     String detail=exception.getClass().getName()+": "+exception.getMessage();
 198:                     sharedWorkEvidence.update("UPDATE shared_market_event_delivery SET last_error=? WHERE source_event_id=? AND analysis_status IN ('RUNNING','REVIEW_REQUIRED')",
 199:                             detail.substring(0,Math.min(1000,detail.length())),sourceEventId);
 200:                 } catch(Exception recordingFailure) {
 201:                     log.error("[FIX-136][ANALYSIS_ERROR_RECORD_FAILED] event={}",sourceEventId,recordingFailure);
 202:                 }
 203:             }
 204:             return "REVIEW_REQUIRED";
 205:         }

 207:     /** FIX-138: emitted only after the transactional service proxy returned.
 208:      * Signal persistence is distinct from wallet processing and delivery completion. */
 209:     private static void logSaved(Long eventId,CandleClosedEvent event,TradeSignal signal,String mode) {
 210:         if(signal==null || signal.getId()==null) {
 211:             log.warn("[FIX-138][SIGNAL_SAVE_UNCONFIRMED] mode={}, event={}, symbol={}, interval={}; service returned no persisted signal identity",
 212:                 mode,eventId,event.symbol(),event.intervalCode());
 213:             return;
 214:         }
 215:         java.time.Instant returned=java.time.Instant.now();
 216:         Long liveDelayMs="LIVE".equals(mode)&&event.closeTime()!=null&&signal.getGeneratedAt()!=null
 217:             ?java.time.Duration.between(event.closeTime(),signal.getGeneratedAt()).toMillis():null;
 218:         Long insertionDelayMs="LIVE".equals(mode)&&event.closeTime()!=null&&signal.getCreatedAt()!=null
 219:             ?java.time.Duration.between(event.closeTime(),signal.getCreatedAt()).toMillis():null;
 220:         log.info("[FIX-153][SIGNAL_SAVED] mode={}, event={}, signal={}, symbol={}, interval={}, candleOpenUtc={}, candleCloseUtc={}, generatedUtc={}, rowCreatedUtc={}, serviceReturnedUtc={}, serviceReturnedKsa={}, liveCloseToGenerationMs={}, liveCloseToInsertionMs={}; rowCreatedUtc is row creation time, not database commit time; historical generatedUtc is an as-of clock, signal save does not imply a wallet trade",
 221:             mode,eventId,signal.getId(),event.symbol(),event.intervalCode(),event.openTime(),event.closeTime(),signal.getGeneratedAt(),signal.getCreatedAt(),returned,returned.atOffset(java.time.ZoneOffset.ofHours(3)),liveDelayMs,insertionDelayMs);
 222:     }
 223:     private String localProcessingOutcome(Long signalId){
 224:         if(signalId==null)return "PROCESSING_UNCONFIRMED";
 225:         var statuses=sharedWorkEvidence.queryForList("SELECT status FROM signal_processing_work WHERE signal_id=?",String.class,signalId);
 226:         if(statuses.size()!=1)return "PROCESSING_UNCONFIRMED";
 227:         return switch(statuses.getFirst()){
 228:             case "COMPLETED"->"COMPLETED";
 229:             case "EXPIRED"->"EXECUTION_EXPIRED";
 230:             default->"PROCESSING_UNCONFIRMED";
 231:         };
 232:     }
 233:     /** FIX-140: Optional.empty is not proof of processing completion. */
 234:     private String processingOutcome(long event) {
 235:         var states=sharedWorkEvidence.queryForList("SELECT status FROM signal_processing_work WHERE source_event_id=?",event);
 236:         if(states.size()!=1)return "REVIEW_REQUIRED";
 237:         return switch(String.valueOf(states.getFirst().get("status"))) {
 238:             case "COMPLETED"->"COMPLETED";
 239:             case "EXPIRED"->"EXECUTION_EXPIRED";
 240:             case "LOCK_RETRY_EXHAUSTED"->"EXECUTION_NOT_EXECUTED";
 241:             case "SYMBOL_LOCK_RETRY"->"EXECUTION_RETRY";
 242:             default->"REVIEW_REQUIRED";
 243:         };
 244:     }
 245: 
 246:     private static void traceStage(Long id, CandleClosedEvent event, String stage, long started) {
 247:         log.info("[FIX-138][WORKER_STAGE] event={}, symbol={}, interval={}, open={}, stage={}, elapsedMs={}",
 248:                 id,event.symbol(),event.intervalCode(),event.openTime(),stage,
 249:                 (System.nanoTime()-started)/1_000_000);
 250:     }
 251:     private String terminal(CandleClosedEvent event,String outcome,String reason,String workerOutcome){
 252:         if(outcomes!=null)outcomes.record(event.symbol(),event.intervalCode(),event.openTime(),outcome,reason,null,"CANDLE_WORKER");
 253:         return workerOutcome;
 254:     }
 255: 
 256: }

--- src/main/java/com/crypto/shared/SharedMarketSource.java
   1: package com.crypto.shared;
   2: 
   3: import com.zaxxer.hikari.HikariConfig;
   4: import com.zaxxer.hikari.HikariDataSource;
   5: import jakarta.annotation.PreDestroy;
   6: import org.springframework.core.env.Environment;
   7: import org.springframework.jdbc.core.JdbcTemplate;
   8: import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
   9: 
  10: /** FIX-132: separate SELECT-only pool; never register a second DataSource bean
  11:  * that would accidentally replace Boot's local Flyway/JPA datasource. */
  12: @Component
  13: public class SharedMarketSource {
  14:     private final boolean enabled;
  15:     private final String mode;
  16:     private final JdbcTemplate local;
  17:     private final JdbcTemplate reader;
  18:     private final HikariDataSource pool;
  19:     public SharedMarketSource(JdbcTemplate local, Environment env) {
  20:         mode = env.getProperty("shared-market.mode", "OFF").trim();
  21:         if (!java.util.Set.of("OFF","OBSERVE","LIVE").contains(mode)) throw new IllegalArgumentException("FIX-132 invalid shared-market.mode");
  22:         this.local=local; enabled="LIVE".equals(mode);
  23:         if ("OFF".equals(mode)) { reader = local; pool = null; return; }
  24:         HikariConfig c = new HikariConfig();
  25:         c.setJdbcUrl(env.getRequiredProperty("shared-market.jdbc-url"));
  26:         c.setUsername(env.getRequiredProperty("shared-market.username"));
  27:         c.setPassword(env.getRequiredProperty("shared-market.password"));
  28:         c.setMaximumPoolSize(3); c.setMinimumIdle(0); c.setReadOnly(true);
  29:         c.setConnectionTimeout(5000); c.setValidationTimeout(2000); c.setConnectionInitSql("SET time_zone = '+00:00'");
  30:         c.setPoolName("fix132-source-read");
  31:         // Finite driver waits as well as pool/query waits; no single overall deadline is claimed.
  32:         c.addDataSourceProperty("connectTimeout", "2000");
  33:         c.addDataSourceProperty("socketTimeout", "5000");
  34:         c.addDataSourceProperty("connectionTimeZone","UTC");
  35:         c.addDataSourceProperty("forceConnectionTimeZoneToSession","true");
  36:         pool = new HikariDataSource(c); reader = new JdbcTemplate(env.getProperty("diagnostics.jdbc.enabled",Boolean.class,true) ? new com.crypto.infrastructure.diagnostics.JdbcTimingDataSource(pool,"collector-read",env.getProperty("diagnostics.jdbc.slow-ms",Long.class,250L)) : pool); reader.setQueryTimeout(5);
  37:         try {
  38:             // FIX-149: validate the explicitly configured collector schema, distinct from the trader database.
  39:             String expectedSchema = env.getProperty("shared-market.source-schema", "crypto_ai_v2");
  40:             if(!expectedSchema.equals(reader.queryForObject("SELECT DATABASE()",String.class)))
  41:                 throw new IllegalStateException("FIX-132 shared connection must target configured source schema " + expectedSchema);
  42:         } catch(RuntimeException failure) { pool.close();throw failure; }
  43:     }
  44:     public boolean enabled() { return enabled; }
  45:     public JdbcTemplate reader() { return enabled ? reader : local; }
  46:     public JdbcTemplate feedReader() { return reader; }
  47:     /** Instantaneous pool pressure, not a per-query connection-wait measurement. */
  48:     public java.util.Map<String,Integer> poolPressure() {
  49:         if(pool==null || pool.getHikariPoolMXBean()==null)return java.util.Map.of();
  50:         var bean=pool.getHikariPoolMXBean();
  51:         return java.util.Map.of("active",bean.getActiveConnections(),"idle",bean.getIdleConnections(),"waiting",bean.getThreadsAwaitingConnection());
  52:     }
  53:     public String mode() { return mode; }
  54:     public void requireLocalWriter() {
  55:         if (enabled) throw new IllegalStateException("FIX-132: collector owns candle writes; Trader import is disabled");
  56:     }
  57:     @PreDestroy public void close() { if (pool != null) pool.close(); }
  58: }

  19: /** FIX-132 independent consumer. The immutable source is never claimed/updated.

  83:     private void sourceStatus(String status,String detail) {
  84:         if(status.equals(lastSourceStatus))return;

  88:     public SharedMarketConsumer(SharedMarketSource source,JdbcTemplate jdbc,CoinConfigurationService coins,
  89:             LivePositionProtectionService protection,PriceMoveMonitorService observer,CandleClosedAnalysisWorker analysis,
  90:             @Qualifier("sharedLiveAnalysisExecutor") Executor executor,
  91:             @Qualifier("sharedHistoricalAnalysisExecutor") Executor historicalExecutor,PlatformTransactionManager manager) {
  92:         this.source=source;this.jdbc=jdbc;this.coins=coins;this.protection=protection;this.observer=observer;
  93:         readinessJdbc=new JdbcTemplate(Objects.requireNonNull(jdbc.getDataSource()));
  94:         readinessJdbc.setQueryTimeout(2);
  95:         readinessTransaction=new TransactionTemplate(manager);
  96:         readinessTransaction.setPropagationBehavior(TransactionDefinition.PROPAGATION_REQUIRES_NEW);
  97:         readinessTransaction.setTimeout(2);
  98:         automaticActivation=new SharedAutomaticActivation(source,readinessJdbc,manager);
  99:         this.analysis=analysis;protocol=new Fix142DeliveryProtocol(jdbc);transaction=new TransactionTemplate(manager);
 100:         analysisDispatch=new SharedAnalysisDispatcher(source,jdbc,coins,
 101:             ()->intervals==null?List.of("1m"):intervals.getIntervals(),analysis,executor,historicalExecutor,manager);
 102:         analysisDispatch.admissionGate(this::symbolAllowed);
 103:         transaction.setPropagationBehavior(TransactionDefinition.PROPAGATION_REQUIRES_NEW);
 104:     }
 105:     @org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Value("${shared-market.activation-approved:false}")
 106:     private boolean activationApproved;
 107:     // FIX-140: disabling history is explicit; candles and saved decision context remain intact.
 108:     @org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Value("${shared-market.analysis.historical-enabled:true}")
 109:     private boolean historicalEnabled=true;
 110:     // FIX-141 Phase 2 review correction: reconcileOwnerTimeoutReviews() no longer mutates
 111:     // analysis_status - it only logs candidates for manual review (see
 112:     // SharedAnalysisDispatcher#reconcileOwnerTimeoutReviews). This flag now gates whether
 113:     // that diagnostic logging runs at all; off by default.
 114:     @org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Value("${shared-market.analysis.owner-timeout-reconciliation-enabled:false}")
 115:     private boolean ownerTimeoutReconciliationEnabled=false;
 116:     @org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Value("${shared-market.analysis.owner-timeout-reconciliation-quiet-seconds:3600}")
 117:     private long ownerTimeoutReconciliationQuietSeconds=3600;
 118:     @org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired
 119:     private com.crypto.client.config.binance.BinanceMarketDataProperties intervals;
 120: 
 121:     @org.springframework.context.event.EventListener(org.springframework.boot.context.event.ApplicationReadyEvent.class)
 122:     public void start() {
 123:         if(fix142Enabled && !source.enabled())throw new IllegalStateException("FIX-142 live scheduling requires LIVE mode");
 124:         if(admissionCeiling<2 || freshReserve<1 || freshReserve>=admissionCeiling)
 125:             throw new IllegalArgumentException("FIX-142 invalid admission ceiling/reserve");
 126:         protocol.ceiling=admissionCeiling;protocol.reserve=freshReserve;
 127:         analysisDispatch.historicalCapacity(historicalThreads);
 128:         analysisDispatch.historicalEnabled(historicalEnabled);
 129:         analysisDispatch.ownerTimeoutReconciliationEnabled(ownerTimeoutReconciliationEnabled);
 130:         analysisDispatch.ownerTimeoutQuietSeconds(ownerTimeoutReconciliationQuietSeconds);
 131:         // FIX-146: do not throw ApplicationReadyEvent for recoverable symbol state.
 132:         // Every dispatch surface uses this gate; diagnostics and operator APIs stay up.
 133:         initializeTradingReadiness();
 134:         analysisDispatch.acknowledgmentEnabled(acknowledgmentEnabled);
 135:         analysisDispatch.freshPriority(fix142Enabled);
 136:         if("OFF".equals(source.mode()))return;
 137:         if(fix142Enabled) {
 138:             freshClock.scheduleWithFixedDelay(this::discoverFresh,0,100,TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS);
 139:             protectionClock.scheduleWithFixedDelay(this::dispatchProtectionTicks,0,50,TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS);
 140:         }
 141:         monitorClock.scheduleWithFixedDelay(analysisDispatch::reconcileCompletedWorkAcknowledgments,60,60,TimeUnit.SECONDS);
 142:         if("OFF".equals(source.mode()))return;
 143:         if(source.enabled())readinessClock.scheduleWithFixedDelay(this::refreshTradingReadiness,10,10,TimeUnit.SECONDS);
 144:         // Independent clocks: slow source reads or CallerRuns analysis saturation
 145:         // cannot monopolize Spring's default scheduler and stop price dispatch.
 146:         discoveryClock.scheduleWithFixedDelay(this::discover,0,100,TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS);
 147:         priceClock.scheduleWithFixedDelay(this::dispatchPrices,0,50,TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS);
 148:         analysisClock.scheduleWithFixedDelay(this::dispatchAnalysis,0,100,TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS);
 149:         if(historicalEnabled)historyClock.scheduleWithFixedDelay(this::dispatchHistoricalAnalysis,1000,1000,TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS);
 150:         log.info("[FIX-140][HISTORY_CONFIGURATION] enabled={}, savedSignalsAndCandlesRetained=true",historicalEnabled);
 151:         monitorClock.scheduleWithFixedDelay(analysisDispatch::monitor,30,30,TimeUnit.SECONDS);
 152:         // FIX-142 review correction: old protection attempts age out of the recent-price
 153:         // selector, so fencing/alerting must not depend on another claim selecting them.
 154:         monitorClock.scheduleWithFixedDelay(this::monitorProtectionAttempts,5,5,TimeUnit.SECONDS);
 155:         // FIX-141 Phase 2: runs independently of claim()'s REVIEW_BATCH_LIMIT check, so a lane
 156:         // that crossed 16 unresolved reviews can still drain over successive runs.
 157:         monitorClock.scheduleWithFixedDelay(analysisDispatch::reconcileAnalysisOnlyReviews,60,60,TimeUnit.SECONDS);
 158:         // FIX-141 Phase 2 review correction: owner-timeout reconciliation is diagnostic-only
 159:         // (logs candidates, performs no mutation) and off until the team enables it and sets a
 160:         // quiet window (see SharedAnalysisDispatcher#ownerTimeoutReconciliationEnabled/Seconds);
 161:         // scheduling it unconditionally here is safe since it no-ops while disabled, and even
 162:         // enabled it cannot change analysis_status itself.
 163:         monitorClock.scheduleWithFixedDelay(analysisDispatch::reconcileOwnerTimeoutReviews,300,300,TimeUnit.SECONDS);
 164:         log.info("[FIX-138][ANALYSIS_CONFIGURATION] liveThreads=8, historyThreads={}, independentClocks=true, livePriority=true, historicalWalletExecution=false",historicalThreads);
 165:     }
 166:     public void validateCutover() {
 167:         log.info("[FIX-132][SOURCE_CONFIGURATION] mode={}, readPool=3, priceWorkers=2, pendingPerSymbol=200, publicationSource=collector",source.mode());
 168:         if(!source.enabled())return;
 169:         if(!activationApproved)throw new IllegalStateException("FIX-132 LIVE requires explicit measured cutover acceptance (activation-approved)");
 170:         var unattributed=jdbc.queryForList(OperatorWorkCancellation.UNATTRIBUTED_BLOCKERS);
 171:         if(!unattributed.isEmpty())throw new IllegalStateException("[FIX-132][UNATTRIBUTED_WORK] reconcile before LIVE: "+unattributed);
 172:         var invalidOwners=jdbc.queryForList("""
 173:             SELECT w.signal_id,w.status,w.source_event_id FROM signal_processing_work w
 174:             LEFT JOIN shared_market_event_delivery d ON d.source_event_id=w.source_event_id
 175:             WHERE w.source_event_id IS NOT NULL AND w.status NOT IN ('COMPLETED','EXPIRED','LOCK_RETRY_EXHAUSTED')
 176:             AND (d.source_event_id IS NULL OR w.symbol<>d.symbol OR w.interval_code<>d.interval_code
 177:                  OR w.candle_open_time<>d.candle_open_time OR d.closed=0
 178:                  OR d.analysis_status NOT IN ('RUNNING','REVIEW_REQUIRED','EXECUTION_RETRY')) ORDER BY w.signal_id LIMIT 25
 179:             """);
 180:         if(!invalidOwners.isEmpty())throw new IllegalStateException("[FIX-132][PROCESSING_OWNER_INCONSISTENT] reconcile before LIVE: "+invalidOwners);
 181:         for(String symbol:coins.enabledSymbols())validateSymbolCutover(symbol);
 182:     }
 183:     // Shared by strict acceptance checks and per-symbol operational readiness.
 184:     private Map<String,Object> validateSymbolCutover(String symbol) {
 185:             var state=readinessJdbc.queryForList("SELECT * FROM shared_market_consumer_state WHERE symbol=?",symbol);
 186:             if(state.isEmpty() || !SharedCutoverPolicy.approved(state.getFirst()))
 187:                 throw new IllegalStateException("FIX-132 missing/unapproved/review-required cutover state for "+symbol);
 188:             var boundary=state.getFirst();
 189:             if(num(boundary,"cutover_sequence")<0 || num(boundary,"discovered_sequence")<num(boundary,"cutover_sequence"))
 190:                 throw new IllegalStateException("FIX-132 invalid sequence boundary for "+symbol);
 191:             Integer approval=readinessJdbc.queryForObject("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM shared_market_cutover_approval WHERE symbol=? AND cutover_sequence=? AND cutover_at=? AND approved_by=? AND approved_at=? AND approval_reference=?",Integer.class,
 192:                 symbol,boundary.get("approved_sequence"),boundary.get("approved_cutover_at"),boundary.get("approved_by"),boundary.get("approved_at"),boundary.get("approval_reference"));
 193:             if(approval==0)throw new IllegalStateException("FIX-132 missing approval audit for "+symbol);
 194:             Long latest=source.feedReader().queryForObject("SELECT last_sequence FROM market_data_stream_cursor WHERE symbol=?",Long.class,symbol);
 195:             if(latest==null || latest<num(state.getFirst(),"discovered_sequence"))throw new IllegalStateException("FIX-132 source sequence regressed for "+symbol);
 196:             Set<String> required=new HashSet<>(intervals.getIntervals());required.add("1m");
 197:             for(String interval:required) {
 198:                 Long count=source.reader().queryForObject("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM (SELECT id FROM candle WHERE symbol=? AND interval_code=? AND closed=1 LIMIT 300) c",Long.class,symbol,interval);
 199:                 if(count==null || count<300)throw new IllegalStateException("FIX-132 incomplete source history: "+symbol+" "+interval);
 200:             }
 201:             return boundary;
 202:     }
 203: 
 204:     private boolean symbolAllowed(String symbol) {
 205:         return !source.enabled() || (readinessSymbols.contains(symbol) || !tradingReadiness.managed())
 206:             && tradingReadiness.allowed(symbol);
 207:     }
 208:     public Map<String,Object> tradingReadiness() {
 209:         var snapshot=new LinkedHashMap<String,Object>(tradingReadiness.snapshot());
 210:         snapshot.put("configuredSymbols",readinessSymbols);
 211:         snapshot.put("automaticFirstCutoverEnabled",automaticFirstCutoverEnabled);
 212:         snapshot.put("waitingCutoverDoesNotAutoApprove",!automaticFirstCutoverEnabled);
 213:         return snapshot;
 214:     }
 215:     void initializeTradingReadiness() {
 216:         if(!source.enabled())return;
 217:         tradingReadiness.begin();
 218:         refreshTradingReadiness();
 219:         log.warn("[FIX-146][STARTUP_READINESS] {}; process stays available, validated first cutovers may activate automatically, uncertain symbols receive no new shared attempts",tradingReadiness.snapshot());
 220:     }
 221:     private static final String SYMBOL_LEGACY_BLOCKERS=OperatorWorkCancellation.UNATTRIBUTED_BLOCKERS
 222:         .replace("WHERE w.source_event_id IS NULL","WHERE w.symbol=? AND w.source_event_id IS NULL");
 223:     private static final String UNSCOPED_BLOCKERS=OperatorWorkCancellation.UNATTRIBUTED_BLOCKERS
 224:         .replace("WHERE w.source_event_id IS NULL","WHERE (w.symbol IS NULL OR TRIM(w.symbol)='') AND w.source_event_id IS NULL");
 225:     // Dedicated clock and round-robin budget: one slow probe cannot monopolize trading
 226:     // clocks or permanently strand later symbols. JDBC timeouts are not total deadlines.
 227:     synchronized void refreshTradingReadiness() {
 228:         if(stopped || !source.enabled())return;
 229:         List<String> symbols;
 230:         try {symbols=List.copyOf(coins.enabledSymbols());}
 231:         catch(RuntimeException failure) {
 232:             for(String symbol:readinessSymbols)recordReadiness(symbol,SharedTradingReadiness.Reason.CHECK_FAILED);
 233:             log.error("[FIX-146][READINESS_INVENTORY_FAILED]",failure);return;
 234:         }
 235:         readinessSymbols=symbols;tradingReadiness.retainSymbols(symbols);
 236:         boolean unscoped;
 237:         try {unscoped=!readinessJdbc.queryForList(UNSCOPED_BLOCKERS).isEmpty()
 238:                 || !readinessJdbc.queryForList("SELECT signal_id FROM signal_processing_work WHERE source_event_id IS NOT NULL AND (symbol IS NULL OR TRIM(symbol)='') AND status NOT IN ('COMPLETED','EXPIRED','LOCK_RETRY_EXHAUSTED') LIMIT 1").isEmpty();}
 239:         catch(RuntimeException failure) {
 240:             for(String symbol:symbols)recordReadiness(symbol,SharedTradingReadiness.Reason.CHECK_FAILED);
 241:             log.error("[FIX-146][READINESS_SCOPE_CHECK_FAILED]",failure);return;
 242:         }
 243:         long deadline=System.nanoTime()+TimeUnit.SECONDS.toNanos(20);
 244:         for(int checked=0;checked<symbols.size();checked++) {
 245:             if(checked>0 && System.nanoTime()>=deadline)break;
 246:             String symbol=symbols.get(Math.floorMod(readinessCursor++,symbols.size()));
 247:             if(unscoped)recordReadiness(symbol,SharedTradingReadiness.Reason.UNSCOPED_WORK);
 248:             else if(!activationApproved)recordReadiness(symbol,SharedTradingReadiness.Reason.ACTIVATION_REQUIRED);
 249:             else {
 250:                 try {
 251:                     if(!readinessJdbc.queryForList(SYMBOL_LEGACY_BLOCKERS,symbol).isEmpty()) {
 252:                         recordReadiness(symbol,SharedTradingReadiness.Reason.LEGACY_WORK_REVIEW);continue;
 253:                     }
 254:                     // Inconsistent cross-symbol ownership blocks both affected symbols.
 255:                     var owners=readinessJdbc.queryForList("""
 256:                         SELECT w.signal_id FROM signal_processing_work w
 257:                         LEFT JOIN shared_market_event_delivery d ON d.source_event_id=w.source_event_id
 258:                         WHERE (w.symbol=? OR d.symbol=?) AND w.source_event_id IS NOT NULL
 259:                           AND w.status NOT IN ('COMPLETED','EXPIRED','LOCK_RETRY_EXHAUSTED')
 260:                           AND (w.status='OPERATOR_CANCELLED' OR d.source_event_id IS NULL OR w.symbol IS NULL
 261:                             OR w.interval_code IS NULL OR w.candle_open_time IS NULL OR d.symbol IS NULL
 262:                             OR d.interval_code IS NULL OR d.candle_open_time IS NULL OR d.closed IS NULL
 263:                             OR d.analysis_status IS NULL OR w.symbol<>d.symbol
 264:                             OR w.interval_code<>d.interval_code OR w.candle_open_time<>d.candle_open_time
 265:                             OR d.closed=0 OR d.analysis_status NOT IN ('RUNNING','REVIEW_REQUIRED','EXECUTION_RETRY'))
 266:                         LIMIT 1
 267:                         """,symbol,symbol);
 268:                     if(!owners.isEmpty()) {recordReadiness(symbol,SharedTradingReadiness.Reason.OWNER_INCONSISTENT);continue;}
 269:                     if(automaticFirstCutoverEnabled)automaticActivation.activateFirst(symbol,intervals.getIntervals());
 270:                     var validated=validateSymbolCutover(symbol);
 271:                     if(!fix142Enabled && !readinessJdbc.queryForList("SELECT symbol FROM shared_market_consumer_state WHERE symbol=? AND active_delivery_state<>'NONE'",symbol).isEmpty()) {
 272:                         recordReadiness(symbol,SharedTradingReadiness.Reason.OWNERSHIP_MODE_MISMATCH);continue;
 273:                     }
 274:                     if(fix142Enabled && !readinessJdbc.queryForList("""
 275:                         SELECT d.source_event_id FROM shared_market_event_delivery d WHERE d.symbol=? AND phase BETWEEN 1 AND 3
 276:                         AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM shared_market_consumer_state s WHERE s.symbol=d.symbol
 277:                           AND s.active_delivery_event_id=d.source_event_id AND s.active_delivery_state IN ('ACTIVE','REVIEW_REQUIRED')) LIMIT 1
 278:                         """,symbol).isEmpty()) {
 279:                         recordReadiness(symbol,SharedTradingReadiness.Reason.PARTIAL_DELIVERY);continue;
 280:                     }
 281:                     // Avoid periodic INSERT/locking for already published history. A changed
 282:                     // boundary is revalidated next pass; dispatch still checks durable approval.
 283:                     Integer history=readinessJdbc.queryForObject("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM shared_market_cutover_history WHERE symbol=? AND cutover_at=? AND cutover_sequence=?",Integer.class,
 284:                         symbol,validated.get("cutover_at"),validated.get("cutover_sequence"));
 285:                     Boolean stillApproved=history!=null && history>0;
 286:                     if(!stillApproved)stillApproved=readinessTransaction.execute(tx->{
 287:                         var state=readinessJdbc.queryForMap("SELECT * FROM shared_market_consumer_state WHERE symbol=? FOR UPDATE",symbol);
 288:                         if(!SharedCutoverPolicy.approved(state))return false;
 289:                         for(String field:List.of("cutover_at","cutover_sequence","approved_sequence","approved_cutover_at","approved_at","approved_by","approval_reference"))
 290:                             if(!Objects.equals(state.get(field),validated.get(field)))return false;
 291:                         readinessJdbc.update("INSERT IGNORE INTO shared_market_cutover_history(symbol,cutover_at,cutover_sequence) VALUES(?,?,?)",
 292:                             symbol,state.get("cutover_at"),state.get("cutover_sequence"));return true;
 293:                     });
 294:                     recordReadiness(symbol,Boolean.TRUE.equals(stillApproved)?SharedTradingReadiness.Reason.READY:SharedTradingReadiness.Reason.CUTOVER_REQUIRED);
 295:                 } catch(RuntimeException failure) {
 296:                     recordReadiness(symbol,readinessReason(failure));
 297:                     log.debug("[FIX-146][READINESS_CHECK_FAILED] symbol={}",symbol,failure);
 298:                 }
 299:             }
 300:             if(System.nanoTime()>=deadline)break;
 301:         }
 302:     }
 303:     private SharedTradingReadiness.Reason readinessReason(RuntimeException failure) {
 304:         String message=String.valueOf(failure.getMessage());
 305:         if(message.contains("missing/unapproved/review-required cutover"))return automaticFirstCutoverEnabled
 306:             ?SharedTradingReadiness.Reason.AUTOMATIC_ACTIVATION_WAITING:SharedTradingReadiness.Reason.CUTOVER_REQUIRED;
 307:         if(message.contains("missing approval audit"))return SharedTradingReadiness.Reason.APPROVAL_AUDIT_MISSING;
 308:         if(message.contains("invalid sequence boundary"))return SharedTradingReadiness.Reason.INVALID_BOUNDARY;
 309:         if(message.contains("source sequence regressed"))return SharedTradingReadiness.Reason.SOURCE_REGRESSED;
 310:         if(message.contains("incomplete source history"))return SharedTradingReadiness.Reason.SOURCE_HISTORY_MISSING;
 311:         return SharedTradingReadiness.Reason.CHECK_FAILED;
 312:     }
 313:     private void recordReadiness(String symbol,SharedTradingReadiness.Reason reason) {
 314:         var before=tradingReadiness.record(symbol,reason);
 315:         if(before==null || before.reason()!=reason) {
 316:             Fix142Metrics.count("READINESS_"+reason.name());
 317:             log.warn("[FIX-146][SYMBOL_READINESS] symbol={}, reason={}, checkedUtc={}, checkedKsa={}; audited first-activation policy, no uncertain retry",
 318:                 symbol,reason,Instant.now(),Instant.now().atOffset(java.time.ZoneOffset.ofHours(3)));
 319:         }
 320:     }
 321: 
 322:     public void discover() {
 323:         if(stopped || "OFF".equals(source.mode())) return;
 324:         try {
 325:             for(String symbol:coins.enabledSymbols()) {
 326:                 if(!symbolAllowed(symbol))continue;
 327:                 var state=jdbc.queryForList("SELECT * FROM shared_market_consumer_state WHERE symbol=?",symbol);
 328:                 if(state.isEmpty()) {
 329:                     if(source.enabled()) { log.error("[FIX-132][REVIEW_REQUIRED] symbol={}; explicit cutover boundary missing",symbol); continue; }
 330:                     var seq=source.feedReader().queryForList("SELECT last_sequence FROM market_data_stream_cursor WHERE symbol=?",symbol);
 331:                     if(seq.isEmpty()) continue;
 332:                     long n=((Number)seq.getFirst().get("last_sequence")).longValue();
 333:                     jdbc.update("INSERT IGNORE INTO shared_market_consumer_state(symbol,cutover_sequence,cutover_at,discovered_sequence,status,cutover_source) VALUES(?,?,CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6),?,'PENDING_CUTOVER','AUTO_OBSERVED')",symbol,n,n);
 334:                     continue;
 335:                 }
 336:                 if(!SharedCutoverPolicy.admitted(state.getFirst(),source.enabled())) continue;
 337:                 int pending=fix142Enabled?-1:jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM shared_market_event_delivery WHERE symbol=? AND phase<4",Integer.class,symbol);
 338:                 boolean discoveryHeartbeat=System.nanoTime()-discoveryLogTimes.getOrDefault(symbol,0L)>=TimeUnit.SECONDS.toNanos(30);
 339:                 if(discoveryHeartbeat)discoveryLogTimes.put(symbol,System.nanoTime());
 340:                 if(!fix142Enabled && pending>=200) {
 341:                     if(discoveryHeartbeat)log.warn("[FIX-132][CONSUMER_LAG] symbol={}, pending={}; discovery paused before analysis admission",symbol,pending);
 342:                     continue;
 343:                 }
 344:                 long after=((Number)state.getFirst().get("discovered_sequence")).longValue();
 345:                 var rows=source.feedReader().queryForList("SELECT * FROM market_data_stream_event WHERE symbol=? AND symbol_sequence>? ORDER BY symbol_sequence LIMIT ?",symbol,after,fix142Enabled?32:Math.min(100,200-pending));
 346:                 log.debug("[FIX-137][DISCOVERY_BATCH] symbol={}, afterSequence={}, pending={}, fetched={}",
 347:                     symbol,after,pending,rows.size());
