14: /** FIX-153: local durable candles close the commit-to-memory-dispatch crash window.
15:  * BACKFILL is never admitted. Legacy NULL provenance is eligible only when the
16:  * live stream wrote a close-transition token.
17:  * Small lookback and one admission per idle lane keep detector below live work. */
18: @Component
19: public class LocalClosedCandleDetector {
20:     private static final Logger log=LoggerFactory.getLogger(LocalClosedCandleDetector.class);
21:     private final JdbcTemplate jdbc;private final CoinConfigurationService coins;
22:     private final CandleAnalysisDispatcher dispatcher;private final CandleAnalysisExecutionCoordinator coordination;
23:     private final com.crypto.client.config.binance.BinanceMarketDataProperties properties;
24:     @Value("${shared-market.mode:OFF}") private String mode;
25:     @org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired(required=false) private ExchangeClockSynchronizer exchangeClock;
26:     @org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired(required=false) private CandleAnalysisOutcomeStore outcomes;
27:     private int nextLane;
28:     private final Map<String,Retry> retries=new HashMap<>();
29:     private final Map<String,Instant> analysedUntil=new HashMap<>();
30:     public LocalClosedCandleDetector(JdbcTemplate jdbc,CoinConfigurationService coins,CandleAnalysisDispatcher dispatcher,CandleAnalysisExecutionCoordinator coordination,com.crypto.client.config.binance.BinanceMarketDataProperties properties){
31:         this.jdbc=new JdbcTemplate(Objects.requireNonNull(jdbc.getDataSource()));this.jdbc.setQueryTimeout(2);
32:         this.coins=coins;this.dispatcher=dispatcher;this.coordination=coordination;this.properties=properties;
33:     }
34:     @Scheduled(initialDelay=5000,fixedDelayString="${local-candle.detector-delay-ms:2000}")
35:     public void detect(){
36:         if(!"OFF".equals(mode))return;
37:         Instant localNow=Instant.now();Instant now=exchangeClock==null?localNow:exchangeClock.correctJvm(localNow);long deadline=System.nanoTime()+java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit.SECONDS.toNanos(2);
38:         try {
39:             var symbols=coins.enabledSymbols();var intervals=properties.getIntervals();
40:             int total=symbols.size()*intervals.size();
41:             for(int checked=0;checked<total;checked++){
42:                 if(System.nanoTime()>deadline)return;
43:                 // FIX-153: rotate across bounded passes so a slow first lane cannot starve later symbols.
44:                 int lane=Math.floorMod(nextLane++,total);
45:                 String symbol=symbols.get(lane/intervals.size()),interval=intervals.get(lane%intervals.size());
46:                 if(!dispatcher.idle(symbol,interval))continue;
47:                 long lookback=Math.max(180,com.crypto.shared.SharedEventPolicy.closeGraceSeconds(interval)*2L);
48:                 // Index is on open_time: include one full interval before the close-age lookback.
49:                 Instant lowerOpen=now.minusSeconds(intervalSeconds(interval)+lookback);
50:                 var candidates=jdbc.query("SELECT open_time,close_time FROM candle WHERE symbol=? AND interval_code=? AND closed=1 AND close_transition_token IS NOT NULL AND (ingestion_source IS NULL OR ingestion_source<>'BACKFILL') AND open_time>=? AND close_time<=? ORDER BY open_time ASC LIMIT 4",
51:                     (rs,n)->new CandleClosedEvent(symbol,interval,rs.getTimestamp(1).toInstant(),rs.getTimestamp(2).toInstant()),symbol,interval,Timestamp.from(lowerOpen),Timestamp.from(now));
52:                 for(var event:candidates){
53:                     String retryKey=key(event);
54:                     Instant knownAnalysedUntil=analysedUntil.get(retryKey);
55:                     if(knownAnalysedUntil!=null && knownAnalysedUntil.isAfter(now))continue;
56:                     Retry retry=retries.get(retryKey);
57:                     if(retry!=null && retry.nextAttempt().isAfter(now))continue;
58:                     // A healthy analysis is terminal for detector purposes. Do this
59:                     // before dispatcher admission so ordinary completed candles never
60:                     // become retry/expiry noise.
61:                     if(!missing(event)){retries.remove(retryKey);analysedUntil.put(retryKey,eligibilityEndsAfter(event,lookback));continue;}
62:                     // No database fence; existing striped candle lock covers only check+handoff.
63:                     var acquired=coordination.tryLock(symbol,interval,event.openTime());
64:                     if(acquired.isEmpty())continue;
65:                     try(var ignored=acquired.get()){
66:                         if(dispatcher.submitDetected(event,()->missing(event))){
67:                             // Admission is not completion. Retain bounded state until
68:                             // durable signal/work evidence appears on a later pass.
69:                             // Every re-admission consumes the same bounded retry budget;
70:                             // a repeatedly failing worker must not execute forever.
71:                             recordAdmission(retryKey,event,now,lookback);
72:                             break;
73:                         }
74:                         defer(retryKey,event,now,"NOT_ADMITTED");
75:                     }
76:                 }
77:             }
78:             for(var iterator=retries.entrySet().iterator();iterator.hasNext();){
79:                 var entry=iterator.next();
80:                 // Retention is strictly after the SQL lower-open-time boundary.
81:                 // Therefore pruning cannot make a still-detectable candle restart
82:                 // with a fresh retry budget.
83:                 if(entry.getValue().retainUntil().isBefore(now)){
84:                     log.info("[FIX-153][DETECTOR_RETRY_PRUNED] candle={}, attempts={}, reason=NO_LONGER_ELIGIBLE",entry.getKey(),entry.getValue().attempts());
85:                     iterator.remove();
86:                 }
87:             }
88:             analysedUntil.entrySet().removeIf(e->!e.getValue().isAfter(now));
89:         }catch(RuntimeException failure){LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.count(LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.Event.DETECTOR_FAILED);
90:             log.warn("[FIX-153][DETECTOR_FAILED] no checkpoint advanced; next bounded pass may retry",failure);}
91:     }
92:     private static String key(CandleClosedEvent e){return e.symbol()+"|"+e.intervalCode()+"|"+e.openTime();}
93:     private void defer(String key,CandleClosedEvent event,Instant now,String reason){
94:         Retry prior=retries.get(key);int attempts=prior==null?1:prior.attempts()+1;
95:         long delay=Math.min(60,1L<<Math.min(6,attempts));
96:         long lookback=Math.max(180,com.crypto.shared.SharedEventPolicy.closeGraceSeconds(event.intervalCode())*2L);
97:         Instant retryDeadline=event.closeTime().plusSeconds(lookback);
98:         Instant retainUntil=eligibilityEndsAfter(event,lookback);
99:         if(attempts>6 || !retryDeadline.isAfter(now)){
100:             // Keep a terminal marker until this candle has aged out of the SQL
101:             // lookback. Removing it immediately would restart the retry budget.
102:             retries.put(key,new Retry(attempts,Instant.MAX,retryDeadline,retainUntil));
103:             recordExpired(event,reason);
104:             log.warn("[FIX-153][DETECTOR_GAP_EXPIRED] candle={}, attempts={}, reason={}",key,attempts,reason);return;
105:         }
106:         retries.put(key,new Retry(attempts,now.plusSeconds(delay),retryDeadline,retainUntil));
107:         log.info("[FIX-153][DETECTOR_RETRY_DEFERRED] candle={}, attempts={}, nextUtc={}, reason={}",key,attempts,now.plusSeconds(delay),reason);
108:     }
109:     private void recordAdmission(String key,CandleClosedEvent event,Instant now,long lookback){
110:         Retry prior=retries.get(key);int attempts=prior==null?1:prior.attempts()+1;
111:         Instant retryDeadline=event.closeTime().plusSeconds(lookback);
112:         Instant retainUntil=eligibilityEndsAfter(event,lookback);
113:         if(attempts>6 || !retryDeadline.isAfter(now)){
114:             // See defer(): this marker is deliberately retained until the query
115:             // can no longer rediscover this candle.
116:             retries.put(key,new Retry(attempts,Instant.MAX,retryDeadline,retainUntil));
117:             recordExpired(event,"ADMISSION_RETRY_LIMIT");
118:             log.warn("[FIX-153][DETECTOR_GAP_EXPIRED] candle={}, attempts={}, reason=ADMISSION_RETRY_LIMIT",key,attempts);
119:             return;
120:         }
121:         long delay=retryDelaySeconds(attempts);
122:         retries.put(key,new Retry(attempts,now.plusSeconds(delay),retryDeadline,retainUntil));
123:         log.info("[FIX-153][DETECTOR_ADMITTED_AWAITING_EVIDENCE] candle={}, attempts={}, nextUtc={}",key,attempts,now.plusSeconds(delay));
124:     }
125:     /** Strictly after the candidate predicate: open_time >= now - interval - lookback. */
126:     private static Instant eligibilityEndsAfter(CandleClosedEvent event,long lookback){
127:         return event.openTime().plusSeconds(intervalSeconds(event.intervalCode())+lookback+1);
128:     }
129:     private static long retryDelaySeconds(int attempts){return Math.min(60,1L<<Math.min(6,attempts));}
130:     private record Retry(int attempts,Instant nextAttempt,Instant retryDeadline,Instant retainUntil) {}
131:     private static long intervalSeconds(String interval){
132:         return switch(interval){case "1m"->60;case "5m"->300;case "15m"->900;case "1h"->3600;case "4h"->14400;case "1d"->86400;
133:             default->throw new IllegalArgumentException("FIX-153 unsupported detector interval: "+interval);};
134:     }
135:     private boolean missing(CandleClosedEvent e){
136:         if(outcomes!=null && outcomes.exists(e.symbol(),e.intervalCode(),e.openTime()))return false;
137:         return jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT (EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM trade_signal WHERE symbol=? AND interval_code=? AND candle_open_time=?) OR EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM signal_processing_work WHERE symbol=? AND interval_code=? AND candle_open_time=?))",Integer.class,
138:             e.symbol(),e.intervalCode(),Timestamp.from(e.openTime()),e.symbol(),e.intervalCode(),Timestamp.from(e.openTime()))==0;
139:     }
140:     private void recordExpired(CandleClosedEvent event,String reason){
141:         if(outcomes!=null)outcomes.record(event.symbol(),event.intervalCode(),event.openTime(),"ANALYSIS_EXPIRED",reason,null,"LOCAL_DETECTOR");
142:     }
143: }

26:         c.setUsername(env.getRequiredProperty("shared-market.username"));

51:         return java.util.Map.of("active",bean.getActiveConnections(),"idle",bean.getIdleConnections(),"waiting",bean.getThreadsAwaitingConnection());
52:     }

54:     public void requireLocalWriter() {
55:         if (enabled) throw new IllegalStateException("FIX-132: collector owns candle writes; Trader import is disabled");
56:     }
57:     @PreDestroy public void close() { if (pool != null) pool.close(); }

---src/main/java/com/crypto/indicator/event/CandleClosedEventListener.java 1-90
1: package com.crypto.indicator.event;
2: 
3: import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
4: import org.springframework.transaction.event.TransactionPhase;
5: import org.springframework.transaction.event.TransactionalEventListener;
6: 
7: /**
8:  * Receives the committed closed-candle event and hands it to FIX-043's ordered async dispatcher.
9:  *
10:  * IMPORTANT: keep this listener intentionally tiny. Before FIX-043 it performed the complete
11:  * indicator -> signal -> wallet chain synchronously inside AFTER_COMMIT. Production evidence on
12:  * 22 Aug 2026 showed ~97-100% candle persistence but only ~17-20% 1m indicator/signal coverage;
13:  * the heavy listener was effectively letting the five-minute recovery scheduler become the
14:  * analysis clock.
15:  *
16:  * We do NOT put @Async directly on this method because a generic async pool can reorder adjacent
17:  * candles (for example 12:02 could start before 12:01). The dispatcher returns immediately to the
18:  * Binance ingestion path while preserving FIFO order per symbol+interval and parallelism across
19:  * independent streams.
20:  */
21: @Component
22: public class CandleClosedEventListener {
23: 
24:     private final CandleAnalysisDispatcher dispatcher;
25: 
26:     public CandleClosedEventListener(CandleAnalysisDispatcher dispatcher) {
27:         this.dispatcher = dispatcher;
28:     }
29: 
30:     @TransactionalEventListener(phase = TransactionPhase.AFTER_COMMIT)
31:     public void handle(CandleClosedEvent event) {
32:         dispatcher.submit(event);
33:     }
34: }

codex-docs\INVESTIGATION_LOG.md-26-- Entry: signal `trade_signal.id=69226` was generated at `2026-10-09T07:33:00.233691Z` (10:33:00.234 KSA) from the 1m candle opened at 10:32 KSA. It was a `BUY`, score 79, confidence 70, entry allowed, at 2502.77. The trade executed at `07:33:01.654548Z` (10:33:01.655 KSA), 1.42 seconds after the signal; this is not an execution-latency issue.
codex-docs\INVESTIGATION_LOG.md-27-- Exit: `wallet_trade.id=60` closed the position at 2504.30 at `07:34:34.196100Z` (10:34:34.196 KSA) with `PROFIT_LOCK_EXIT`. The managed position recorded a high of 2505.80 and an activated profit-lock price of 2504.429502243252. The realized paper gain was about 0.02445 USDT before fees (0.015982291620 ETH x 1.53 USDT).
codex-docs\INVESTIGATION_LOG.md-28-- Market path: ETH rose from 2485.88 at 07:54 KSA to 2502.77 at entry (+16.89, +0.68%). It reached 2505.56 at 10:25 KSA before the entry; entry was 2.79 (0.11%) below that high. The post-entry high was 2505.93 at 10:34 KSA, only 3.16 (0.13%) above entry, then the candle retraced. This supports the user's observation that entry occurred late in the observed move.
codex-docs\INVESTIGATION_LOG.md:29:- Primary cause: the local candle-quality interruption stopped 1m ETH signals after 07:34 KSA. There were no ETH 1m signals from 07:34 to 10:26 KSA, the period containing most of the rise. The interrupted 1m and 5m rows were repaired at about 10:28 KSA, so analysis resumed only near the later consolidation/breakout attempt. This is a confirmed data-availability gap, not proof that the strategy would have bought near 07:54 KSA.
codex-docs\INVESTIGATION_LOG.md-30-- Immediate signal sequence after recovery: at 10:30 KSA the 5m signal was a BUY but blocked by `RANGE_ENTRY_LOCATION`; at 10:31 KSA the 1m raw BUY was vetoed by a bearish BTC context; at 10:33 KSA BTC context became neutral and the 1m trend-following BUY was allowed. The entry signal itself explicitly recorded mixed higher-timeframe context (5m BUY, 1h NEUTRAL) and a weakening but ultra-close ask wall before its target.
codex-docs\INVESTIGATION_LOG.md:31:- Assessment: the trade followed the implemented rules once local analysis resumed, and the trade was entered/exited promptly. It was nevertheless a late entry relative to the full 07:54–10:33 KSA advance because the system lacked signal coverage during the main move. The data gap is an operational defect already covered by the local-candle recovery deployment gate. Whether the unobserved earlier period would have produced an executable BUY is uncertain without a controlled Replay run; none was started because that would create replay state.

## FINDINGS

1. The checked-in default is trader-owned ingestion: `shared-market.mode=OFF`. In this mode the trader opens Binance kline WebSockets, performs REST bootstrap/imports, and persists to local `crypto_ai.candle`.

2. Collector/shared-market ownership remains implemented, not removed. In `LIVE`, the trader connects read-only to `crypto_ai_v2` and shared executors consume collector stream/cursor tables. `OBSERVE` also creates that external collector connection and starts shared clocks.

3. `OBSERVE` is architecturally contradictory: direct WebSocket/REST ingestion is disabled only for `LIVE`, while shared executors run in every non-`OFF` mode. Thus OBSERVE runs both direct ingestion and shared-market processing, contrary to AGENTS.md’s pending rule that they must not run together.

4. Runtime WebSocket candle updates are monotonic: completed candles are not overwritten; a transition token is assigned only on the SQL open-to-closed transition. The transaction order is input → protection → observer → close-event publication; dispatch occurs after commit.

5. Ordinary REST import/backfill is unsafe relative to the pending design. It calls a generic `BACKFILL` duplicate-key upsert. Against an existing open runtime row, it updates the OHLCV data and changes `closed` to `1`, but does not create a runtime close token, dispatch analysis, or write reconciliation/outcome evidence. The nearby comment that backfill “inserts missing identities only” contradicts the SQL.

6. Therefore, the reported live-close interleaving is substantiated by code: REST can close an open runtime row first; a later final WebSocket event cannot install its token because `closed=1`; normal close dispatch does not occur. Reconciliation subsequently excludes that row because it only selects `closed=0`; the local detector requires a token. This is a material admission gap.

7. A dedicated reconciliation implementation exists and is substantially closer to the pending direction: it uses exchange time plus grace, update-only recovery for an existing open candle, insert-if-absent for gaps, audit/outcome records, and no live analysis dispatch. However, it is disabled by default.

8. Even when enabled, reconciliation is incomplete against AGENTS.md requirements: the open-candidate query limits rows before the due/loss-evidence filters; reconnect evidence is process-local; no demonstrated shared Binance 429/418/ban admission control exists across reconciliation, bootstrap, clock sync, and other REST callers.

9. Dispatch admission is not fully durable. The dispatcher can replace pending 1m work or reject higher-timeframe work under bounded queue pressure. The detector can rediscover tokened candles, but only while the lane is idle and within a bounded lookback. Its retry state is in memory; a restart or prolonged busy lane can let an uncompleted candle age out without a durable terminal outcome.

10. The detector’s `missing()` considers an existing `trade_signal` sufficient completion even if subsequent signal processing fails. The worker persists the signal before `paperTradingService.processSignal`; its general failure path returns `REVIEW_REQUIRED`. The code does not establish that every such local failure has terminal durable work/outcome evidence.

11. Historical-only recovery is behaviorally separated but not structurally isolated: the same `CandleClosedAnalysisWorker` that selects `analyzeRecovered` also injects and invokes `PaperTradingService` for live processing. This fails the stronger AGENTS.md requirement that historical-only paths be structurally unable to reach execution services.

12. Replay does not demonstrate ingestion/recovery parity. The normal replay loader does not filter `closed`; the dataset forward path does. Neither shown path simulates close tokens, provenance, reconciliation grace/retries/audits, dispatcher saturation, or recovery admission.

13. Production behavior is unknown. This was a repository-only review; no production systems, credentials, databases, runtime processes, deployed overrides, or logs were accessed.

- AGENTS.md’s unconditional V103 fresh-install blocker conflicts with the checked-in B105 baseline’s stated trader-only treatment: [AGENTS.md](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/AGENTS.md:71), [B105__fix_153_trader_only_baseline.sql](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/resources/db/migration/B105__fix_153_trader_only_baseline.sql:1). This is documentation/design drift, not proof that a fresh installation passes.

## OBJECTIONS

- Current ordinary REST import violates the pending recovery constraints and can consume live-close analysis admission.
- OBSERVE violates the no-dual-ingestion direction.
- Default configuration leaves specialized recovery disabled.
- Durable analysis admission, restart handling, terminal-outcome coverage, and Replay parity are not proven.
- Historical-only structural isolation and shared REST rate-limit/ban handling are absent or unproven.
- The implementation does not meet AGENTS.md’s deployment-readiness gates.

Candidate for later solution review only: route ordinary import away from duplicate-key finalisation of runtime-open rows, or otherwise give it the same grace/audit/no-token semantics as specialized reconciliation.

## UNKNOWNS

- Whether the import/live-close race has occurred in an actual deployment.
- Whether and how deployed configuration overrides `OFF`.
- Whether local MySQL concurrency/crash tests and fresh B105 bootstrap have passed.
- Whether local `processSignal` failure always leaves independently durable processing evidence.
- Exact production collector availability, release artifact, database/Flyway state, and replacement-time position protection.
